Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5110 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A floor dip with hands behind you and hips lifted. It trains the triceps with just your bodyweight.

Proposed staging

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on the floor with knees bent and hands behind you. 5110.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lift your hips off the floor. 5110.json#/instructions/1
  3. Bend your elbows to lower your hips. 5110.json#/instructions/2
  4. Push through your palms to straighten your arms. 5110.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your elbows pointing back. 5110.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keep your shoulders away from your ears. 5110.json#/instructions/5

5118 — cable twist v. 2

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5118 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Rotate your torso against a cable. It strengthens the obliques and builds control of your trunk rotation.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Set a cable at chest height and stand sideways to it. Hold the handle with both hands in front of your chest.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5118.json#/instructions/0, 5118.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold the handle with both hands in front of your chest.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 5118.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 5118.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set a cable at chest height and stand sideways to it. 5118.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the handle with both hands in front of your chest. 5118.json#/instructions/1
  3. Rotate your torso away from the cable, arms extended. 5118.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your hips facing forward. 5118.json#/instructions/3
  5. Return slowly, then switch sides after your reps. 5118.json#/instructions/4

5119 — dumbbell deadlift v. 2

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5119 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Lift dumbbells from the floor by hinging at the hips. It strengthens your glutes, hamstrings and back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with feet hip-width apart, dumbbells at your sides or in front of your thighs.

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5121 after decompression) · Source record

5122 — barbell clean and jerk

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5122 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Lift a barbell from the floor to your shoulders, then drive it overhead. A technical full-body lift for power and speed.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set up over the bar with a flat back and hands outside your knees. 5122.json#/instructions/0
  2. Pull the bar off the floor and catch it at your shoulders in a squat. 5122.json#/instructions/1

  4. Dip slightly and drive the bar overhead, splitting your feet. 5122.json#/instructions/3

5124 — side jump twist

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5124 after decompression) · Source record

Jump while twisting your hips and legs to the side. It warms up the core and legs.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet together and arms bent. 5124.json#/instructions/0
  2. Jump and twist your hips and legs to one side while your shoulders face forward. 5124.json#/instructions/1
